entry-content container-lg\" itemprop=\"text\"\u003e\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch1 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eContributing to GitHub Agentic Workflows\u003c/h1\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-contributing-to-github-agentic-workflows\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Contributing to GitHub Agentic Workflows\" href=\"#contributing-to-github-agentic-workflows\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThank you for your interest in contributing to GitHub Agentic Workflows! We welcome contributions from the community and are excited to work with you.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cg-emoji class=\"g-emoji\" alias=\"warning\"\u003e⚠️\u003c/g-emoji\u003e IMPORTANT: This project uses agentic development by a core team (inner-circle) primarily using Copilot coding agent or local coding agents.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e🚫 Traditional Pull Requests Are Not Enabled for non-Core team members\u003c/strong\u003e: If you are not part of the core team, please do not create pull requests directly. Instead, you create detailed agentic plans in issues, discuss with the team, and a core team member will create and implement the PR for you using agents.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis document deals with the contribution process for non-Core team members.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePrerequisites\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-prerequisites\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Prerequisites\" href=\"#prerequisites\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cg-emoji class=\"g-emoji\" alias=\"warning\"\u003e⚠️\u003c/g-emoji\u003e \u003cstrong\u003eGeneric dev environments (e.g. manually installed Node.js, Go, or other tools) are not supported.\u003c/strong\u003e\nThis project is designed to be developed inside a \u003cstrong\u003eDev Container\u003c/strong\u003e or \u003cstrong\u003eGitHub Codespace\u003c/strong\u003e, which automatically configures all required tools and runtimes.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe recommended way to set up a development environment is to use the provided \u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/.devcontainer/devcontainer.json\"\u003eDev Container\u003c/a\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGitHub Codespaces\u003c/strong\u003e (recommended): Open this repository in a Codespace — everything is pre-configured automatically, including Go, Node.js 24, Docker, and the GitHub CLI.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eVS Code Dev Container\u003c/strong\u003e: Install the \u003ca href=\"https://marketplace.visualstudio.com/items?itemName=ms-vscode-remote.remote-containers\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eDev Containers extension\u003c/a\u003e, then open the repository folder and choose \u003cstrong\u003eReopen in Container\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe Dev Container installs all required dependencies and runs \u003ccode\u003emake deps\u003c/code\u003e automatically on creation. No manual setup is needed.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you encounter errors about Node.js or Go versions when running \u003ccode\u003emake deps\u003c/code\u003e or other build commands, this is a sign that you are not using the Dev Container. Please switch to a Dev Container or Codespace environment.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eKeep local agent settings private\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-keep-local-agent-settings-private\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Keep local agent settings private\" href=\"#keep-local-agent-settings-private\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eClaude Code can persist plaintext credentials from approved commands in \u003ccode\u003e.claude/settings.local.json\u003c/code\u003e. Repository ignore rules exclude this file at every project depth. Builds and the \u003cstrong\u003eAgent local settings security\u003c/strong\u003e PR check reject indexed copies, including files added with \u003ccode\u003egit add -f\u003c/code\u003e. Run \u003ccode\u003emake test-agent-local-settings\u003c/code\u003e to check the repository and exercise the guard.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf the check fails, use \u003ccode\u003egit rm --cached -- \u0026lt;path\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e to untrack each reported file without deleting the local copy. Rotate any credentials already published: untracking does not erase Git history or forks. Shared \u003ccode\u003e.claude/settings.json\u003c/code\u003e files and skills remain allowed, but must not contain credentials. This guard checks the current Git index; it is not a general secret scanner or a history audit.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🤖 How Development Works\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--how-development-works\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🤖 How Development Works\" href=\"#-how-development-works\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eGitHub Agentic Workflows is developed by a core team using agentic development — primarily GitHub Copilot coding agent and local coding agents. This means:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e✅ \u003cstrong\u003eCore team uses agents to create and manage pull requests\u003c/strong\u003e - via Copilot coding agent or local agents\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e✅ \u003cstrong\u003eAutomated quality assurance\u003c/strong\u003e - CI runs all checks on all PRs\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e✅ \u003cstrong\u003eCommunity contributes through agentic plans\u003c/strong\u003e - you craft the plan, the core team executes it\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e❌ \u003cstrong\u003eTraditional pull requests from non-core members are not enabled\u003c/strong\u003e - contribute through issues instead\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhy This Approach?\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-why-this-approach\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Why This Approach?\" href=\"#why-this-approach\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis project practices what it preaches: agentic development is used to build agentic workflows. Benefits include:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eConsistency\u003c/strong\u003e: All changes go through the same automated quality gates\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDogfooding\u003c/strong\u003e: We use our own tools to build our tools\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBest practices\u003c/strong\u003e: Agents follow established patterns and guidelines automatically\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQuality plans\u003c/strong\u003e: Encourages contributors to think through the full implementation before work begins\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🚀 Quick Start for Community Contributors\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--quick-start-for-community-contributors\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🚀 Quick Start for Community Contributors\" href=\"#-quick-start-for-community-contributors\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cg-emoji class=\"g-emoji\" alias=\"warning\"\u003e⚠️\u003c/g-emoji\u003e \u003cstrong\u003eIf you are not part of the core team, do not create pull requests directly.\u003c/strong\u003e Instead, craft a detailed agentic plan in an issue and a core team member will pick it up and implement it using agents.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eStep 1: Analyze with an Agent (for bug reports)\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-step-1-analyze-with-an-agent-for-bug-reports\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Step 1: Analyze with an Agent (for bug reports)\" href=\"#step-1-analyze-with-an-agent-for-bug-reports\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBefore filing a contribution request\u003c/strong\u003e, use an agent to:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eScan the source code to identify root causes (for bugs)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAnalyze execution patterns and trace the issue\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eResearch similar issues and solutions\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePropose specific fixes with code examples\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCreate a comprehensive plan for the changes needed\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eStep 2: Open an Issue with Your Agentic Plan\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-step-2-open-an-issue-with-your-agentic-plan\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Step 2: Open an Issue with Your Agentic Plan\" href=\"#step-2-open-an-issue-with-your-agentic-plan\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCreate an issue\u003c/strong\u003e with your detailed agentic plan:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDescribe what you want to contribute\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInclude your agent's analysis and findings (for bugs)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eExplain the use case and expected behavior\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eProvide a \u003cstrong\u003ecomplete, step-by-step plan\u003c/strong\u003e for the agent to follow\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInclude specific implementation details and examples\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTag with appropriate labels (see \u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/labels.md\"\u003eLabel Guidelines\u003c/a\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSee \u003ca href=\"#reporting-issues-and-feature-requests\"\u003eReporting Issues and Feature Requests\u003c/a\u003e for complete guidelines.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExample agentic plan in an issue:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-text-md notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"## Add support for custom MCP server timeout configuration\n\n### Analysis\nThe current MCP server configuration lacks a timeout field, which can cause workflows to hang indefinitely when servers don't respond.\n\n### Implementation Plan\nPlease implement the following changes:\n\n1. **Update Schema** (`pkg/parser/schemas/frontmatter.json`):\n   - Add a `timeout` field to MCP server configuration schema\n   - Type: integer\n   - Range: 5-300 seconds\n   - Default: 30 seconds\n\n2. **Update Validation** (`pkg/workflow/mcp_validation.go`):\n   - Add validation for timeout values between 5-300 seconds\n   - Use error message format: \u0026quot;[what's wrong]. [what's expected]. [example]\u0026quot;\n   - Example error: \u0026quot;timeout value 400 exceeds maximum. Expected value between 5-300 seconds. Example: timeout: 60\u0026quot;\n\n3. **Add Tests** (`pkg/workflow/mcp_validation_test.go`):\n   - Test valid timeout values (5, 30, 300)\n   - Test invalid timeout values (0, 4, 301, 1000)\n   - Test missing timeout (should use default)\n\n4. **Update Documentation** (`docs/src/content/docs/reference/frontmatter.md`):\n   - Add timeout field to MCP server configuration examples\n   - Explain timeout behavior and defaults\n   - Show example with custom timeout value\n\n5. **Follow Guidelines**:\n   - Use console formatting from `pkg/console` for CLI output\n   - Follow error message style guide\n   - Run `make agent-report-progress` once before the final push\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e## \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eAdd support for custom MCP server timeout configuration\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e### \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eAnalysis\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\nThe current MCP server configuration lacks a timeout field, which can cause workflows to hang indefinitely when servers don't respond.\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e### \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eImplementation Plan\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\nPlease implement the following changes:\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eUpdate Schema\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e (\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003epkg/parser/schemas/frontmatter.json\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e):\n   \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e Add a \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etimeout\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e field to MCP server configuration schema\n   \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e Type: integer\n   \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e Range: 5-300 seconds\n   \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e Default: 30 seconds\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e2\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eUpdate Validation\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e (\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003epkg/workflow/mcp_validation.go\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e):\n   \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e Add validation for timeout values between 5-300 seconds\n   \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e[\u003c/span\u003ewhat's expected\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e]\u003c/span\u003e. \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e[\u003c/span\u003eexample\u003cspan agentic plan and use a coding agent (Copilot or local) to implement it\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRead relevant documentation and specifications\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMake code changes following established patterns\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRun \u003ccode\u003emake agent-report-progress\u003c/code\u003e once to validate changes before the final push\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCreate a PR and handle review feedback and adjustments\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eYou don't create or own the PR\u003c/strong\u003e — the core team member does, using agents as their implementation tool.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e📝 How to Contribute as a Community Member\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--how-to-contribute-as-a-community-member\" class=\"anchor\" \u003cstrong\u003eagentic plans in GitHub issues\u003c/strong\u003e. A core team member then picks up the issue and uses a coding agent to implement it in a pull request.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eHow the Process Works\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-how-the-process-works\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: How the Process Works\" href=\"#how-the-process-works\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eYou create an issue\u003c/strong\u003e with a detailed agentic plan describing what needs to be done\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCore team reviews\u003c/strong\u003e your plan and may ask questions or suggest refinements\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eA core team member picks it up\u003c/strong\u003e and uses a coding agent to implement your plan\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eThe agent follows your instructions\u003c/strong\u003e and handles the technical details\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCore team reviews the PR\u003c/strong\u003e and provides feedback\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAgent iterates\u003c/strong\u003e based on review comments until approved\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePR is merged\u003c/strong\u003e when all checks pass and reviews are satisfied\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eYou do not create pull requests yourself.\u003c/strong\u003e Instead, you craft comprehensive plans that a core team member executes using agents.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat the Implementing Agent Handles\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-what-the-implementing-agent-handles\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What the Implementing Agent Handles\" will:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRead specifications\u003c/strong\u003e from \u003ccode\u003escratchpad/\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eskills/\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003e.github/instructions/\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFollow code organization patterns\u003c/strong\u003e (see \u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/code-organization.md\"\u003escratchpad/code-organization.md\u003c/a\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eImplement validation\u003c/strong\u003e following the architecture in \u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/validation-architecture.md\"\u003escratchpad/validation-architecture.md\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUse console formatting\u003c/strong\u003e from \u003ccode\u003epkg/console\u003c/code\u003e for CLI output\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWrite error messages\u003c/strong\u003e following the \u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/.github/skills/error-messages/SKILL.md\"\u003eError Message Style Guide\u003c/a\u003e and \u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/docs/src/content/docs/troubleshooting/error-messages.md\"\u003edocs guide\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRun the final quality gate\u003c/strong\u003e: \u003ccode\u003emake agent-report-progress\u003c/code\u003e (build, impacted tests, workflow drift, formatting, lint)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUpdate documentation\u003c/strong\u003e for new features\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCreate tests\u003c/strong\u003e for new functionality\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv research\u003c/strong\u003e. This accelerates implementation and helps maintainers focus on high-quality contributions.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🤖 Use Agents for Bug Analysis\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--use-agents-for-bug-analysis\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🤖 Use Agents for Bug Analysis\" href=\"#-use-agents-for-bug-analysis\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBug reports submitted with minimal analysis or research are likely to be ignored.\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUse an agent to analyze the source code, identify root causes, propose fixes, and research similar issues before filing a bug report.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🐛 Debugging Workflow Failures\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--debugging-workflow-failures\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🐛 Debugging Workflow Failures\" href=\"#-debugging-workflow-failures\"\u003e\u003csvg 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor workflow failures, use this prompt with your agent:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-text-md notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"Please debug this workflow failure:\nhttps://github.com/owner/repo/actions/runs/RUN_ID\n\nFollow https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/debug.md and investigate:\n- Why the workflow failed\n- What tools were missing\n- How to fix the configuration\n\nGenerate an investigation report and a plan to address the issue for an agent.\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003ePlease debug this workflow failure:\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-corl\"\u003ehttps://github.com/owner/repo/actions/runs/RUN_ID\u003c/span\u003e\n\nFollow \u003cspan class=\"pl-corl\"\u003ehttps://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/debug.md\u003c/span\u003e and investigate:\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e Why the workflow failed\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e What tools were missing\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e How to fix the configuration\n\nGenerate an investigation report and a plan to address the issue for an agent.\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe agent will use \u003ccode\u003egh aw\u003c/code\u003e or the mcp server to analyze the failure. See \u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/.github/aw/debug-agentic-workflow.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.github/aw/debug-agentic-workflow.md\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e for details.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e📝 Issue Guidelines\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--issue-guidelines\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 📝 Issue Guidelines\" href=\"#-issue-guidelines\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen filing issues with agentic plans:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBugs\u003c/strong\u003e: Include thorough agent analysis, root cause, proposed fix, and detailed implementation plan\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFeatures\u003c/strong\u003e: Explain the use case, provide examples, suggest implementation approach with step-by-step instructions\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWorkflow failures\u003c/strong\u003e: Debug with agents first, then report with analysis and remediation plan\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eImplementation details\u003c/strong\u003e: Be specific about file changes, function names, validation rules, test cases\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eComplete plans\u003c/strong\u003e: The more detailed your plan, the easier it is for the core team to execute it with an agent\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFollow \u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/labels.md\"\u003eLabel Guidelines\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA core team member will pick up the issue and implement your plan using a coding agent\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQuality of the agentic plan directly impacts implementation success.\u003c/strong\u003e Provide comprehensive, step-by-step instructions with specific details.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e✍️ Writing Prompts for Copilot-Assigned Issues\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-️-writing-prompts-for-copilot-assigned-issues\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: ✍️ Writing Prompts for Copilot-Assigned Issues\" href=\"#️-writing-prompts-for-copilot-assigned-issues\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen an issue will be assigned to Copilot coding agent (or another SWE agent), how you write the task description measurably affects whether the resulting PR merges. Recurring \"Copilot PR Prompt Pattern Analysis\" reports (see \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/github/gh-aw/discussions/53203\" data-hovercard-type=\"discussion\" data-hovercard-url=\"/github/gh-aw/discussions/53203/hovercard\"\u003ediscussion #53203\u003c/a\u003e) show a consistent trend: merged PRs average \u003cstrong\u003e~151 words\u003c/strong\u003e per task prompt and name concrete files or subsystems (e.g. \u003ccode\u003epkg\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eengine\u003c/code\u003e, a specific package), while closed/unsuccessful PRs average \u003cstrong\u003e~229 words\u003c/strong\u003e — roughly 50% longer — and skew toward open-ended, exploratory language without a concrete acceptance criterion.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo improve merge rate for agent-assigned work:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBe concise.\u003c/strong\u003e Aim for a short, focused prompt rather than a long, exploratory one. Prompts longer than ~200 words without a concrete acceptance criterion correlate with lower merge rates.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eName concrete files or subsystems.\u003c/strong\u003e Reference specific packages, files, or functions (e.g. \u003ccode\u003epkg/workflow\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecmd/gh-aw\u003c/code\u003e) instead of describing the problem in the abstract.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eState an explicit acceptance criterion.\u003c/strong\u003e Describe what \"done\" looks like (e.g. \"the CLI flag \u003ccode\u003e--foo\u003c/code\u003e is parsed and validated\" or \"add a test in \u003ccode\u003epkg/parser/parser_test.go\u003c/code\u003e covering X\") instead of open-ended language like \"investigate and fix as appropriate.\"\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAvoid pure exploration prompts.\u003c/strong\u003e Prompts that only ask the agent to \"look into\" or \"figure out\" a problem without a concrete target tend to result in longer, less-scoped PRs that are more likely to be closed.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNote: automated CVE/dependency-tracker PRs are excluded from this signal, since their close rate reflects triage policy rather than prompt quality.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCode Quality Standards\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-code-quality-standards\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Code Quality Standards\" href=\"#code-quality-standards\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eError Messages\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-error-messages\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Error Messages\" href=\"#error-messages\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAll validation errors follow the template: \u003cstrong\u003e[what's wrong]. [what's expected]. [example]\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"// Agent produces error messages like this:\nreturn fmt.Errorf(\u0026quot;invalid time delta format: +%s. Expected format like +25h, +3d, +1w, +1mo. Example: +3d\u0026quot;, deltaStr)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// Agent produces error messages like this:\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003ereturn\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003efmt\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eErrorf\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"invalid time delta format: +%s. Expected format like +25h, +3d, +1w, +1mo. Example: +3d\"\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003edeltaStr\u003c/span\u003e)\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe agent runs \u003ccode\u003emake lint-errors\u003c/code\u003e to verify error message quality.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eConsole Output\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-console-output\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Console Output\" href=\"#console-output\"\u003e\u003csvg 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 uses styled console functions from \u003ccode\u003epkg/console\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import \u0026quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/console\u0026quot;\n\nfmt.Println(console.FormatSuccessMessage(\u0026quot;Operation completed\u0026quot;))\nfmt.Println(console.FormatInfoMessage(\u0026quot;Processing workflow...\u0026quot;))\nfmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, console.FormatErrorMessage(err.Error()))\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/console\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003efmt\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ePrintln\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003econsole\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eFormatSuccessMessage\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"Operation completed\"\u003c/span\u003e))\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003efmt\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ePrintln\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003econsole\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eFormatInfoMessage\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"Processing workflow...\"\u003c/span\u003e))\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003efmt\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eFprintln\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eos\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eStderr\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003econsole\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eFormatErrorMessage\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eerr\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eError\u003c/span\u003e()))\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eFile Organization\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-file-organization\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: File Organization\" href=\"#file-organization\"\u003e\u003csvg 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe agent follows these principles:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePrefer many small files\u003c/strong\u003e over large monolithic files\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGroup by functionality\u003c/strong\u003e, not by type\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUse descriptive names\u003c/strong\u003e that clearly indicate purpose\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFollow established patterns\u003c/strong\u003e from the codebase\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKey Patterns the Agent Uses\u003c/strong\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCreate Functions Pattern\u003c/strong\u003e - One file per GitHub entity creation\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eExamples: \u003ccode\u003ecreate_issue.go\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecreate_pull_request.go\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecreate_discussion.go\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEngine Separation Pattern\u003c/strong\u003e - Each engine has its own file\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eExamples: \u003ccode\u003ecopilot_engine.go\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eclaude_engine.go\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecodex_engine.go\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eShared helpers in \u003ccode\u003eengine_helpers.go\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFocused Utilities Pattern\u003c/strong\u003e - Self-contained feature files\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eExamples: \u003ccode\u003eexpressions.go\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003estrings.go\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eartifacts.go\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSee \u003ca attacks:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUse \u003ccode\u003efileutil.ValidateAbsolutePath\u003c/code\u003e before file operations:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import \u0026quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/fileutil\u0026quot;\n\n// Validate path before reading/writing files\ncleanPath, err := fileutil.ValidateAbsolutePath(userInputPath)\nif err != nil {\n    return fmt.Errorf(\u0026quot;invalid path: %w\u0026quot;, err)\n}\ncontent, err := os.ReadFile(cleanPath)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/fileutil\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// Validate path before reading/writing files\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ecleanPath\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eerr\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e:=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003efileutil\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eValidateAbsolutePath\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003euserInputPath\u003c/span\u003e)\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eif\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eerr\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e!=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003enil\u003c/span\u003e {\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003ereturn\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003efmt\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eErrorf\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"invalid path: %w\"\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eerr\u003c/span\u003e)\n}\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003econtent\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eerr\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e:=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eos\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eReadFile\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ecleanPath\u003c/span\u003e)\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSecurity checks performed:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNormalizes path using \u003ccode\u003efilepath.Clean\u003c/code\u003e (removes \u003ccode\u003e.\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e..\u003c/code\u003e components)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eVerifies path is absolute (blocks relative path traversal)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eReturns descriptive errors for invalid paths\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhen to use:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBefore \u003ccode\u003eos.ReadFile\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eos.WriteFile\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eos.Stat\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eos.Open\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBefore \u003ccode\u003eos.MkdirAll\u003c/code\u003e or other directory operations\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAfter constructing paths with \u003ccode\u003efilepath.Join\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhen processing user-provided file paths\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis provides defense-in-depth against path traversal vulnerabilities (e.g., \u003ccode\u003e../../../etc/passwd\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCLI output structure, altering defaults\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNon-breaking\u003c/strong\u003e: Adding new commands/flags, adding output fields, bug fixes\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor breaking changes, the agent:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUses \u003ccode\u003emajor\u003c/code\u003e changeset type\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eProvides migration guidance\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDocuments in CHANGELOG.md\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSee \u003ca 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAll community-sourced pull requests are created and managed by core team members using coding agents:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCreate an issue with your agentic plan:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOpen an issue describing what needs to be done in detail\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eProvide a complete, step-by-step implementation plan\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInclude clear context, examples, and specific technical details\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTag appropriately using \u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/labels.md\"\u003eLabel Guidelines\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCore team reviews and engages:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA core team member reviews your agentic plan\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThey may ask questions, suggest changes, or refine the approach\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIf the plan looks good, a core team member picks it up\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCore team member creates and implements the PR using an agent:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThey run the plan through a coding agent (Copilot or local)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe agent reads specifications and guidelines\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe agent makes changes following established patterns\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe agent runs \u003ccode\u003emake agent-report-progress\u003c/code\u003e once before the final push\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAutomated quality checks:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCI runs on all PRs\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAll checks must pass (build, test, lint, recompile)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe core team member addresses any CI failures\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eReview and iterate:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCore team reviews the PR\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eProvide feedback as comments\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAgent-assisted revisions are made as needed\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOnce approved, PR is merged\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePR dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCreate the pull request as \u003cstrong\u003edraft\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMove it to \u003cstrong\u003eReady for review\u003c/strong\u003e and approve required CI workflows.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRun the \u003ccode\u003epr-finisher\u003c/code\u003e skill (automates final review/check/mergeability hardening) to get to green.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor features that deeply impact the engine, add the \u003ccode\u003esmoke\u003c/code\u003e label and approve workflows.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIf no smoke run is queued after setting \u003ccode\u003esmoke\u003c/code\u003e, or additional changes require another smoke run, toggle the \u003ccode\u003esmoke\u003c/code\u003e label (remove and re-add) and approve workflows again.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePR Triage Labels (Core Team)\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-pr-triage-labels-core-team\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: PR Triage Labels (Core Team)\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePR triage assigns one risk label (\u003ccode\u003epr-risk:low\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003epr-risk:medium\u003c/code\u003e, or\n\u003ccode\u003epr-risk:high\u003c/code\u003e), a priority label (\u003ccode\u003epr-priority:*\u003c/code\u003e), and one action label.\nPriority reflects impact, urgency, and quality; quality includes CI status,\ntest coverage, and the PR description.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003epr-action:fast_track\u003c/code\u003e identifies a high-value, high-priority PR that is\nready for expedited human review. The risk label determines the review\nscope; fast-track does not bypass required CI or approvals.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003epr-action:batch_review\u003c/code\u003e groups similar low- or medium-risk PRs for review\ntogether, typically when a cluster contains three or more related PRs.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003epr-action:defer\u003c/code\u003e identifies lower-value, lower-priority work that remains\nopen but is not currently prioritized.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRemember: As a community contributor, you don't create the PR yourself.\u003c/strong\u003e You create an issue with a detailed plan, discuss it with the team, and a core team member creates the PR using agents.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat Gets Validated\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-what-gets-validated\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What Gets Validated\" href=\"#what-gets-validated\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEvery agent-created PR automatically runs:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emake build\u003c/code\u003e - Ensures Go code compiles\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emake test\u003c/code\u003e - Runs all unit and integration tests\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emake lint\u003c/code\u003e - Checks code quality and style\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emake recompile\u003c/code\u003e - Recompiles all workflows to ensure compatibility. If you edit \u003ccode\u003e.github/workflows/*.md\u003c/code\u003e, run this before committing so the paired \u003ccode\u003e.lock.yml\u003c/code\u003e files stay in sync with CI.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emake fmt\u003c/code\u003e - Formats changed Go, JavaScript, TypeScript, and JSON files; use \u003ccode\u003emake fmt-all\u003c/code\u003e for full-repository formatting\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emake lint-errors\u003c/code\u003e - Validates error message quality\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor local iteration, run \u003ccode\u003emake fmt\u003c/code\u003e and one focused test command. Before an\nintermediate agent save, \u003ccode\u003emake agent-report-progress-no-test\u003c/code\u003e formats changes\nand compiles/vets affected Go packages without running test bodies, package-wide\nGo linters, or full workflow recompilation. Run \u003ccode\u003emake agent-report-progress\u003c/code\u003e\nonce before the final push to retain the build, lint, impacted-test, and workflow\ndrift checks. Cold Go compilation and final validation can take longer than the\nwarm inner loop.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" following licenses are \u003cstrong\u003enot allowed\u003c/strong\u003e as they conflict with our MIT license or impose unacceptable restrictions:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGPL, LGPL, AGPL\u003c/strong\u003e - Copyleft licenses that would force us to release under GPL\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSSPL\u003c/strong\u003e - Server Side Public License with restrictive requirements\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eProprietary/Commercial\u003c/strong\u003e - Closed-source licenses requiring payment or special terms\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eContainer Base OS Packages\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-container-base-os-packages\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Container Base OS Packages\" href=\"#container-base-os-packages\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe license policy in \u003ccode\u003e.grant.yaml\u003c/code\u003e is also applied to the container images referenced by compiled workflows (\u003ccode\u003egh aw compile --grant\u003c/code\u003e). Packages that ship with the upstream base images are listed under \u003ccode\u003eignore-packages\u003c/code\u003e as a documented exception: the Alpine base OS packages (\u003ccode\u003ebusybox\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eapk-tools\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ealpine-baselayout\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003emusl-utils\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003egit\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003elibgcc\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003elibstdc++\u003c/code\u003e, and their variants), the Debian base OS packages of the \u003ccode\u003eghcr.io/github/github-mcp-server\u003c/code\u003e image (\u003ccode\u003ebase-files\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003elibc6\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003elibssl3\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003emedia-types\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003enetbase\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003etzdata\u003c/code\u003e), and the Node.js/npm runtime with npm's bundled dependencies (\u003ccode\u003enode\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003enpm\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003etar\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eglob\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eminipass\u003c/code\u003e, and friends). They are executed as part of a third-party image, never linked into or redistributed with gh-aw, and cannot be changed without replacing the upstream image. Every other package in those images is still evaluated against the allowlist above.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.grant.yaml\u003c/code\u003e also supports an \u003ccode\u003eignore-images\u003c/code\u003e list of glob patterns matched against the image reference. It excludes an entire image from license scanning and is reserved for third-party images whose bundled toolchains produce license findings that cannot be acted on, such as \u003ccode\u003eghcr.io/oraios/serena\u003c/code\u003e (a Debian development image whose cargo registry ships hundreds of crates without SPDX license metadata). This key is specific to gh-aw; grant ignores it. Vulnerability scanning still covers every image, including the ignored ones.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003elibatomic\u003c/code\u003e is an explicitly approved runtime-package exception for the vendored \u003ccode\u003eghcr.io/github/gh-aw-node\u003c/code\u003e image. The verified Linux/amd64 image inherited Alpine 3.24's \u003ccode\u003elibatomic@15.2.0-r5\u003c/code\u003e unchanged from its Node base. Its \u003ca href=\"https://pkgs.alpinelinux.org/package/v3.24/main/x86_64/libatomic\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eAlpine package metadata\u003c/a\u003e declares \u003ccode\u003eGPL-2.0-or-later AND LGPL-2.1-or-later\u003c/code\u003e, inherited from the GCC packaging recipe; \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/gcc-mirror/gcc/blob/releases/gcc-15.2.0/libatomic/gload.c#L1-L24\"\u003eGCC 15.2 libatomic source notices\u003c/a\u003e specify \u003ccode\u003eGPL-3.0-or-later WITH GCC-exception-3.1\u003c/code\u003e. This is a policy acceptance, not a finding that publishing the runtime has no license obligations: the \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/gcc-mirror/gcc/blob/releases/gcc-15.2.0/COPYING.RUNTIME\"\u003eGCC Runtime Library Exception\u003c/a\u003e does not waive applicable notice or source-distribution requirements for the library itself. Grant's \u003ccode\u003eignore-packages\u003c/code\u003e mechanism applies by package name across scanned images and versions. The general license allowlist, vulnerability scanning, and vendored-image gates remain unchanged.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eContainer Vulnerability Exceptions\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-container-vulnerability-exceptions\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Container Vulnerability Exceptions\" href=\"#container-vulnerability-exceptions\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003egh aw compile --grype\u003c/code\u003e reads the optional \u003ccode\u003e.grype.yaml\u003c/code\u003e file at the repository root and applies its \u003ccode\u003eignore\u003c/code\u003e rules. The file is reserved for documented risk acceptances: findings that have no fixed version available upstream, where there is nothing to upgrade to. Each rule is scoped to a specific vulnerability ID, package, and affected version so newly disclosed vulnerabilities and rebuilt packages are still reported, and each carries a \u003ccode\u003ereason\u003c/code\u003e explaining the acceptance. Remove a rule as soon as the upstream image ships a fix — the daily container scan runs \u003ccode\u003egh aw compile --force-refresh-container-pins\u003c/code\u003e and picks up rebuilt base images automatically. When \u003ccode\u003e.grype.yaml\u003c/code\u003e is absent, grype runs with its defaults.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eBefore Adding a Dependency\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-before-adding-a-dependency\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Before Adding a Dependency\" href=\"#before-adding-a-dependency\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eGitHub Copilot Agent automatically checks licenses when adding dependencies. However, if you're evaluating a dependency:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCheck its license\u003c/strong\u003e: Run \u003ccode\u003emake license-check\u003c/code\u003e after adding the dependency\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eReview the report\u003c/strong\u003e: Run \u003ccode\u003emake license-report\u003c/code\u003e to generate a CSV of all licenses\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIf unsure\u003c/strong\u003e: Ask in your PR - maintainers will help evaluate edge cases\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eLicense Checking\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-license-checking\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: License Checking\" href=\"#license-checking\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe project includes automated license compliance checking:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCI Workflow\u003c/strong\u003e: \u003ccode\u003e.github/workflows/license-check.yml\u003c/code\u003e runs on every PR that changes \u003ccode\u003ego.mod\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLocal Check\u003c/strong\u003e: Run \u003ccode\u003emake license-check\u003c/code\u003e to verify all dependencies (installs \u003ccode\u003ego-licenses\u003c/code\u003e on-demand)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLicense Report\u003c/strong\u003e: Run \u003ccode\u003emake license-report\u003c/code\u003e to see detailed license information\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAll dependencies are automatically scanned using Google's \u003ccode\u003ego-licenses\u003c/code\u003e tool in CI, which classifies licenses by type and identifies potential compliance issues. Note that \u003ccode\u003ego-licenses\u003c/code\u003e is not actively maintained, so we install it on-demand rather than as a regular build dependency.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🤖 Automated Dependency Updates (Dependabot)\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--automated-dependency-updates-dependabot\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🤖 Automated Dependency Updates (Dependabot)\" security patches and version updates.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat Dependabot is configured in \u003ccode\u003e.github/dependabot.yml\u003c/code\u003e to monitor:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGo modules\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e/go.mod\u003c/code\u003e) - Weekly updates for Go dependencies\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003enpm packages\u003c/strong\u003e - Weekly updates for:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDocumentation site (\u003ccode\u003e/docs/package.json\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eGitHub Actions setup scripts (\u003ccode\u003e/actions/setup/js/package.json\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWorkflow dependencies (\u003ccode\u003e/.github/workflows/package.json\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePython packages\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e/.github/workflows/requirements.txt\u003c/code\u003e) - Weekly updates for workflow scripts\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eExpected Behavior\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-expected-behavior\" class=\"anchor\" \u003cstrong\u003eevery Monday\u003c/strong\u003e (weekly interval)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePull Requests\u003c/strong\u003e: Creates automated PRs from \u003ccode\u003edependabot[bot]\u003c/code\u003e for:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSecurity vulnerabilities (immediate)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eVersion updates (weekly batch)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLimit\u003c/strong\u003e: Maximum of 10 open PRs per ecosystem to prevent overwhelming maintainers\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat to Expect from Dependabot PRs\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-what-to-expect-from-dependabot-prs\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What to Expect from Dependabot PRs\" like \"Bump lodash from 4.17.20 to 4.17.21 in /docs\"\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInclude changelog links and release notes\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eShow compatibility score based on semantic versioning\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAutomatically rebase when the base branch changes\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eTroubleshooting Dependabot\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-troubleshooting-dependabot\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf Dependabot stops creating PRs:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCheck repository settings\u003c/strong\u003e: Go to Settings → Security → Dependabot\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEnsure \"Dependabot alerts\" is enabled\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEnsure \"Dependabot security updates\" is enabled\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEnsure \"Dependabot version updates\" is enabled\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eVerify configuration\u003c/strong\u003e: Check \u003ccode\u003e.github/dependabot.yml\u003c/code\u003e syntax\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDirectory paths must match locations of dependency files\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEcosystem names must be exact: \u003ccode\u003egomod\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003enpm\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003epip\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCheck for rate limits\u003c/strong\u003e: Dependabot may be rate-limited if there are too many updates\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eManual trigger\u003c/strong\u003e: You can manually trigger Dependabot from repository Settings → Security → Dependabot\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eHandling Dependabot PRs\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-handling-dependabot-prs\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Handling Dependabot PRs\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eReview the changes\u003c/strong\u003e: Check the changelog and compatibility score\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLet CI run\u003c/strong\u003e: Wait for all GitHub Actions checks to pass\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTest if needed\u003c/strong\u003e: For major version updates, test locally or let the agent verify\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMerge quickly\u003c/strong\u003e: Security updates should be merged as soon as CI passes\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBatch updates\u003c/strong\u003e: For minor version updates, you can merge multiple PRs at once\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSecurity Patches\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-security-patches\" 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDependabot prioritizes security patches:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSecurity vulnerabilities are updated \u003cstrong\u003eimmediately\u003c/strong\u003e (not weekly)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePRs are tagged with severity level (critical, high, medium, low)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSecurity PRs should be reviewed and merged within 24-48 hours\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🧪 Testing\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--testing\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🧪 Testing\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor comprehensive testing guidelines including assert vs require usage, table-driven test patterns, and best practices, see \u003cstrong\u003e\u003ca href=\"/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/testing.md\"\u003escratchpad/testing.md\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eQuick reference:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emake test-unit\u003c/code\u003e - Impacted Go tests (30-second selected-runtime budget; compilation is additional)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emake test-unit-all\u003c/code\u003e - Full Go unit test suite\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emake agent-report-progress\u003c/code\u003e - Final branch-scoped validation before pushing\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emake agent-finish\u003c/code\u003e - Explicit repository-wide maintenance sweep; not an inner-loop command\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCompiling against a different actions repository\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-compiling-against-a-different-actions-repository\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Compiling against a different actions repository\" href=\"#compiling-against-a-different-actions-repository\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen working on changes that span both \u003ccode\u003egithub/gh-aw\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003egithub/gh-aw-actions\u003c/code\u003e, you can compile workflows against a fork or branch of the actions repository:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Compile against a fork with a specific branch\n./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \\\n  --actions-repo myorg/my-aw-actions \\\n  --action-tag my-feature-branch \\\n  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\n\n# Compile with a specific tag or SHA\n./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \\\n  --action-tag abc123def456 \\\n  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Compile against a fork with a specific branch\u003c/span\u003e\n./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \\\n  --actions-repo myorg/my-aw-actions \\\n  --action-tag my-feature-branch \\\n  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Compile with a specific tag or SHA\u003c/span\u003e\n./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \\\n  --action-tag abc123def456 \\\n  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--action-mode action\u003c/code\u003e — Required with \u003ccode\u003e--actions-repo\u003c/code\u003e. References actions as GitHub Actions from an external repository instead of inlining scripts locally.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--actions-repo \u0026lt;owner/repo\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e — Override the default \u003ccode\u003egithub/gh-aw-actions\u003c/code\u003e repository.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--action-tag \u0026lt;tag-or-sha\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e — Pin action references to a specific tag, branch, or commit SHA.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUse these flags when testing workflow behavior against a branch in \u003ccode\u003egithub/gh-aw-actions\u003c/code\u003e before a release is cut.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 right to clean up spam, unsolicited promotions, or off-topic content as needed to keep discussions focused and valuable for all contributors.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis includes but is not limited to:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRepeated identical or similar comments across multiple issues or pull requests\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUnsolicited promotional content or advertisements\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOff-topic comments that don't contribute to the discussion\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAutomated bot comments without prior approval\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🤝 Community\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca
