   <span class="pl-v">-</span> Run <span class="pl-s">`</span><span class="pl-c1">make agent-report-progress</span><span class="pl-s">`</span> once before the final push</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Step 3: Discuss and Refine with the Team</h3><a id="user-content-step-3-discuss-and-refine-with-the-team" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Step 3: Discuss and Refine with the Team" href="#step-3-discuss-and-refine-with-the-team"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Once you've opened the issue:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Core team reviews your plan</strong>: A core team member will look at your issue and may ask clarifying questions</li>
<li><strong>Iterate on the plan</strong>: Discuss and refine the implementation approach based on team feedback</li>
<li><strong>Plan gets approved</strong>: A core team member signals they'll pick it up</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Step 4: A Core Team Member Implements the PR</h3><a id="user-content-step-4-a-core-team-member-implements-the-pr" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Step 4: A Core Team Member Implements the PR" href="#step-4-a-core-team-member-implements-the-pr"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">A core team member will:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Take your agentic plan and use a coding agent (Copilot or local) to implement it</li>
<li>Read relevant documentation and specifications</li>
<li>Make code changes following established patterns</li>
<li>Run <code>make agent-report-progress</code> once to validate changes before the final push</li>
<li>Create a PR and handle review feedback and adjustments</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>You don't create or own the PR</strong> — the core team member does, using agents as their implementation tool.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📝 How to Contribute as a Community Member</h2><a id="user-content--how-to-contribute-as-a-community-member" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📝 How to Contribute as a Community Member" href="#-how-to-contribute-as-a-community-member"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">All community contributions flow through <strong>agentic plans in GitHub issues</strong>. A core team member then picks up the issue and uses a coding agent to implement it in a pull request.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How the Process Works</h3><a id="user-content-how-the-process-works" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How the Process Works" href="#how-the-process-works"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>You create an issue</strong> with a detailed agentic plan describing what needs to be done</li>
<li><strong>Core team reviews</strong> your plan and may ask questions or suggest refinements</li>
<li><strong>A core team member picks it up</strong> and uses a coding agent to implement your plan</li>
<li><strong>The agent follows your instructions</strong> and handles the technical details</li>
<li><strong>Core team reviews the PR</strong> and provides feedback</li>
<li><strong>Agent iterates</strong> based on review comments until approved</li>
<li><strong>PR is merged</strong> when all checks pass and reviews are satisfied</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>You do not create pull requests yourself.</strong> Instead, you craft comprehensive plans that a core team member executes using agents.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What the Implementing Agent Handles</h3><a id="user-content-what-the-implementing-agent-handles" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What the Implementing Agent Handles" href="#what-the-implementing-agent-handles"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When a core team member implements your plan, the coding agent they use will:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Read specifications</strong> from <code>scratchpad/</code>, <code>skills/</code>, and <code>.github/instructions/</code></li>
<li><strong>Follow code organization patterns</strong> (see <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/code-organization.md">scratchpad/code-organization.md</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Implement validation</strong> following the architecture in <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/validation-architecture.md">scratchpad/validation-architecture.md</a></li>
<li><strong>Use console formatting</strong> from <code>pkg/console</code> for CLI output</li>
<li><strong>Write error messages</strong> following the <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/.github/skills/error-messages/SKILL.md">Error Message Style Guide</a> and <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/docs/src/content/docs/troubleshooting/error-messages.md">docs guide</a></li>
<li><strong>Run the final quality gate</strong>: <code>make agent-report-progress</code> (build, impacted tests, workflow drift, formatting, lint)</li>
<li><strong>Update documentation</strong> for new features</li>
<li><strong>Create tests</strong> for new functionality</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Reporting Issues and Feature Requests</h3><a id="user-content-reporting-issues-and-feature-requests" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Reporting Issues and Feature Requests" href="#reporting-issues-and-feature-requests"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Before filing an issue, <strong>use an agent to perform thorough analysis and research</strong>. This accelerates implementation and helps maintainers focus on high-quality contributions.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🤖 Use Agents for Bug Analysis</h4><a id="user-content--use-agents-for-bug-analysis" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🤖 Use Agents for Bug Analysis" href="#-use-agents-for-bug-analysis"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Bug reports submitted with minimal analysis or research are likely to be ignored.</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">Use an agent to analyze the source code, identify root causes, propose fixes, and research similar issues before filing a bug report.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🐛 Debugging Workflow Failures</h4><a id="user-content--debugging-workflow-failures" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🐛 Debugging Workflow Failures" href="#-debugging-workflow-failures"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For workflow failures, use this prompt with your agent:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-md notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Please debug this workflow failure:
https://github.com/owner/repo/actions/runs/RUN_ID

Follow https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/debug.md and investigate:
- Why the workflow failed
- What tools were missing
- How to fix the configuration

Generate an investigation report and a plan to address the issue for an agent."><pre>Please debug this workflow failure:
<span class="pl-corl">https://github.com/owner/repo/actions/runs/RUN_ID</span>

Follow <span class="pl-corl">https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/main/debug.md</span> and investigate:
<span class="pl-v">-</span> Why the workflow failed
<span class="pl-v">-</span> What tools were missing
<span class="pl-v">-</span> How to fix the configuration

Generate an investigation report and a plan to address the issue for an agent.</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The agent will use <code>gh aw</code> or the mcp server to analyze the failure. See <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/.github/aw/debug-agentic-workflow.md"><code>.github/aw/debug-agentic-workflow.md</code></a> for details.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📝 Issue Guidelines</h4><a id="user-content--issue-guidelines" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📝 Issue Guidelines" href="#-issue-guidelines"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When filing issues with agentic plans:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Bugs</strong>: Include thorough agent analysis, root cause, proposed fix, and detailed implementation plan</li>
<li><strong>Features</strong>: Explain the use case, provide examples, suggest implementation approach with step-by-step instructions</li>
<li><strong>Workflow failures</strong>: Debug with agents first, then report with analysis and remediation plan</li>
<li><strong>Implementation details</strong>: Be specific about file changes, function names, validation rules, test cases</li>
<li><strong>Complete plans</strong>: The more detailed your plan, the easier it is for the core team to execute it with an agent</li>
<li>Follow <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/labels.md">Label Guidelines</a></li>
<li>A core team member will pick up the issue and implement your plan using a coding agent</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Quality of the agentic plan directly impacts implementation success.</strong> Provide comprehensive, step-by-step instructions with specific details.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">✍️ Writing Prompts for Copilot-Assigned Issues</h4><a id="user-content-️-writing-prompts-for-copilot-assigned-issues" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ✍️ Writing Prompts for Copilot-Assigned Issues" href="#️-writing-prompts-for-copilot-assigned-issues"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When an issue will be assigned to Copilot coding agent (or another SWE agent), how you write the task description measurably affects whether the resulting PR merges. Recurring "Copilot PR Prompt Pattern Analysis" reports (see <a href="https://github.com/github/gh-aw/discussions/53203" data-hovercard-type="discussion" data-hovercard-url="/github/gh-aw/discussions/53203/hovercard">discussion #53203</a>) show a consistent trend: merged PRs average <strong>~151 words</strong> per task prompt and name concrete files or subsystems (e.g. <code>pkg</code>, <code>engine</code>, a specific package), while closed/unsuccessful PRs average <strong>~229 words</strong> — roughly 50% longer — and skew toward open-ended, exploratory language without a concrete acceptance criterion.</p>
<p dir="auto">To improve merge rate for agent-assigned work:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Be concise.</strong> Aim for a short, focused prompt rather than a long, exploratory one. Prompts longer than ~200 words without a concrete acceptance criterion correlate with lower merge rates.</li>
<li><strong>Name concrete files or subsystems.</strong> Reference specific packages, files, or functions (e.g. <code>pkg/workflow</code>, <code>cmd/gh-aw</code>) instead of describing the problem in the abstract.</li>
<li><strong>State an explicit acceptance criterion.</strong> Describe what "done" looks like (e.g. "the CLI flag <code>--foo</code> is parsed and validated" or "add a test in <code>pkg/parser/parser_test.go</code> covering X") instead of open-ended language like "investigate and fix as appropriate."</li>
<li><strong>Avoid pure exploration prompts.</strong> Prompts that only ask the agent to "look into" or "figure out" a problem without a concrete target tend to result in longer, less-scoped PRs that are more likely to be closed.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Note: automated CVE/dependency-tracker PRs are excluded from this signal, since their close rate reflects triage policy rather than prompt quality.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Code Quality Standards</h3><a id="user-content-code-quality-standards" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Code Quality Standards" href="#code-quality-standards"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Core team members and the agents they use follow these standards:</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Error Messages</h4><a id="user-content-error-messages" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Error Messages" href="#error-messages"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">All validation errors follow the template: <strong>[what's wrong]. [what's expected]. [example]</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Agent produces error messages like this:
return fmt.Errorf(&quot;invalid time delta format: +%s. Expected format like +25h, +3d, +1w, +1mo. Example: +3d&quot;, deltaStr)"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Agent produces error messages like this:</span>
<span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Errorf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"invalid time delta format: +%s. Expected format like +25h, +3d, +1w, +1mo. Example: +3d"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">deltaStr</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The agent runs <code>make lint-errors</code> to verify error message quality.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Console Output</h4><a id="user-content-console-output" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Console Output" href="#console-output"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The agent uses styled console functions from <code>pkg/console</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import &quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/console&quot;

fmt.Println(console.FormatSuccessMessage(&quot;Operation completed&quot;))
fmt.Println(console.FormatInfoMessage(&quot;Processing workflow...&quot;))
fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, console.FormatErrorMessage(err.Error()))"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s">"github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/console"</span>

<span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Println</span>(<span class="pl-s1">console</span>.<span class="pl-c1">FormatSuccessMessage</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Operation completed"</span>))
<span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Println</span>(<span class="pl-s1">console</span>.<span class="pl-c1">FormatInfoMessage</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Processing workflow..."</span>))
<span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Fprintln</span>(<span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Stderr</span>, <span class="pl-s1">console</span>.<span class="pl-c1">FormatErrorMessage</span>(<span class="pl-s1">err</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Error</span>()))</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">File Organization</h4><a id="user-content-file-organization" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: File Organization" href="#file-organization"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The agent follows these principles:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Prefer many small files</strong> over large monolithic files</li>
<li><strong>Group by functionality</strong>, not by type</li>
<li><strong>Use descriptive names</strong> that clearly indicate purpose</li>
<li><strong>Follow established patterns</strong> from the codebase</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Key Patterns the Agent Uses</strong>:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Create Functions Pattern</strong> - One file per GitHub entity creation</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Examples: <code>create_issue.go</code>, <code>create_pull_request.go</code>, <code>create_discussion.go</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Engine Separation Pattern</strong> - Each engine has its own file</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Examples: <code>copilot_engine.go</code>, <code>claude_engine.go</code>, <code>codex_engine.go</code></li>
<li>Shared helpers in <code>engine_helpers.go</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Focused Utilities Pattern</strong> - Self-contained feature files</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Examples: <code>expressions.go</code>, <code>strings.go</code>, <code>artifacts.go</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/code-organization.md">Code Organization Patterns</a> for details.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Validation Patterns</h4><a id="user-content-validation-patterns" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Validation Patterns" href="#validation-patterns"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The agent places validation logic appropriately:</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Centralized validation</strong> (<code>pkg/workflow/validation.go</code>):</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Cross-cutting concerns</li>
<li>Core workflow integrity</li>
<li>GitHub Actions compatibility</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Domain-specific validation</strong> (dedicated files):</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>strict_mode_validation.go</code> - Security enforcement</li>
<li><code>pip_validation.go</code> - Python packages</li>
<li><code>npm_validation.go</code> - NPM packages</li>
<li><code>docker_validation.go</code> - Docker images</li>
<li><code>expression_safety.go</code> - Expression security</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/validation-architecture.md">Validation Architecture</a> for the complete decision tree.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">File Path Security</h4><a id="user-content-file-path-security" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: File Path Security" href="#file-path-security"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">All file operations must validate paths to prevent path traversal attacks:</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Use <code>fileutil.ValidateAbsolutePath</code> before file operations:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-go notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import &quot;github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/fileutil&quot;

// Validate path before reading/writing files
cleanPath, err := fileutil.ValidateAbsolutePath(userInputPath)
if err != nil {
    return fmt.Errorf(&quot;invalid path: %w&quot;, err)
}
content, err := os.ReadFile(cleanPath)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s">"github.com/github/gh-aw/pkg/fileutil"</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Validate path before reading/writing files</span>
<span class="pl-s1">cleanPath</span>, <span class="pl-s1">err</span> <span class="pl-c1">:=</span> <span class="pl-s1">fileutil</span>.<span class="pl-c1">ValidateAbsolutePath</span>(<span class="pl-s1">userInputPath</span>)
<span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-s1">err</span> <span class="pl-c1">!=</span> <span class="pl-c1">nil</span> {
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">fmt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Errorf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"invalid path: %w"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">err</span>)
}
<span class="pl-s1">content</span>, <span class="pl-s1">err</span> <span class="pl-c1">:=</span> <span class="pl-s1">os</span>.<span class="pl-c1">ReadFile</span>(<span class="pl-s1">cleanPath</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Security checks performed:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Normalizes path using <code>filepath.Clean</code> (removes <code>.</code> and <code>..</code> components)</li>
<li>Verifies path is absolute (blocks relative path traversal)</li>
<li>Returns descriptive errors for invalid paths</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Before <code>os.ReadFile</code>, <code>os.WriteFile</code>, <code>os.Stat</code>, <code>os.Open</code></li>
<li>Before <code>os.MkdirAll</code> or other directory operations</li>
<li>After constructing paths with <code>filepath.Join</code></li>
<li>When processing user-provided file paths</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">This provides defense-in-depth against path traversal vulnerabilities (e.g., <code>../../../etc/passwd</code>).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CLI Breaking Changes</h4><a id="user-content-cli-breaking-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CLI Breaking Changes" href="#cli-breaking-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The agent evaluates whether changes are breaking:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Breaking</strong>: Removing/renaming commands or flags, changing JSON output structure, altering defaults</li>
<li><strong>Non-breaking</strong>: Adding new commands/flags, adding output fields, bug fixes</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">For breaking changes, the agent:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Uses <code>major</code> changeset type</li>
<li>Provides migration guidance</li>
<li>Documents in CHANGELOG.md</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/breaking-cli-rules.md">Breaking CLI Rules</a> for details.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔄 Pull Request Process for Community Contributions</h2><a id="user-content--pull-request-process-for-community-contributions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔄 Pull Request Process for Community Contributions" href="#-pull-request-process-for-community-contributions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">All community-sourced pull requests are created and managed by core team members using coding agents:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Create an issue with your agentic plan:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Open an issue describing what needs to be done in detail</li>
<li>Provide a complete, step-by-step implementation plan</li>
<li>Include clear context, examples, and specific technical details</li>
<li>Tag appropriately using <a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/labels.md">Label Guidelines</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Core team reviews and engages:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>A core team member reviews your agentic plan</li>
<li>They may ask questions, suggest changes, or refine the approach</li>
<li>If the plan looks good, a core team member picks it up</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Core team member creates and implements the PR using an agent:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>They run the plan through a coding agent (Copilot or local)</li>
<li>The agent reads specifications and guidelines</li>
<li>The agent makes changes following established patterns</li>
<li>The agent runs <code>make agent-report-progress</code> once before the final push</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Automated quality checks:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>CI runs on all PRs</li>
<li>All checks must pass (build, test, lint, recompile)</li>
<li>The core team member addresses any CI failures</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Review and iterate:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Core team reviews the PR</li>
<li>Provide feedback as comments</li>
<li>Agent-assisted revisions are made as needed</li>
<li>Once approved, PR is merged</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">PR Lifecycle Tip (Core Team)</h3><a id="user-content-pr-lifecycle-tip-core-team" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: PR Lifecycle Tip (Core Team)" href="#pr-lifecycle-tip-core-team"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When implementing community contributions using an agent:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Create the pull request as <strong>draft</strong>.</li>
<li>Move it to <strong>Ready for review</strong> and approve required CI workflows.</li>
<li>Run the <code>pr-finisher</code> skill (automates final review/check/mergeability hardening) to get to green.</li>
<li>For features that deeply impact the engine, add the <code>smoke</code> label and approve workflows.</li>
<li>If no smoke run is queued after setting <code>smoke</code>, or additional changes require another smoke run, toggle the <code>smoke</code> label (remove and re-add) and approve workflows again.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">PR Triage Labels (Core Team)</h3><a id="user-content-pr-triage-labels-core-team" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: PR Triage Labels (Core Team)" href="#pr-triage-labels-core-team"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">PR triage assigns one risk label (<code>pr-risk:low</code>, <code>pr-risk:medium</code>, or
<code>pr-risk:high</code>), a priority label (<code>pr-priority:*</code>), and one action label.
Priority reflects impact, urgency, and quality; quality includes CI status,
test coverage, and the PR description.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>pr-action:fast_track</code> identifies a high-value, high-priority PR that is
ready for expedited human review. The risk label determines the review
scope; fast-track does not bypass required CI or approvals.</li>
<li><code>pr-action:batch_review</code> groups similar low- or medium-risk PRs for review
together, typically when a cluster contains three or more related PRs.</li>
<li><code>pr-action:defer</code> identifies lower-value, lower-priority work that remains
open but is not currently prioritized.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Remember: As a community contributor, you don't create the PR yourself.</strong> You create an issue with a detailed plan, discuss it with the team, and a core team member creates the PR using agents.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What Gets Validated</h3><a id="user-content-what-gets-validated" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What Gets Validated" href="#what-gets-validated"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Every agent-created PR automatically runs:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>make build</code> - Ensures Go code compiles</li>
<li><code>make test</code> - Runs all unit and integration tests</li>
<li><code>make lint</code> - Checks code quality and style</li>
<li><code>make recompile</code> - Recompiles all workflows to ensure compatibility. If you edit <code>.github/workflows/*.md</code>, run this before committing so the paired <code>.lock.yml</code> files stay in sync with CI.</li>
<li><code>make fmt</code> - Formats changed Go, JavaScript, TypeScript, and JSON files; use <code>make fmt-all</code> for full-repository formatting</li>
<li><code>make lint-errors</code> - Validates error message quality</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">For local iteration, run <code>make fmt</code> and one focused test command. Before an
intermediate agent save, <code>make agent-report-progress-no-test</code> formats changes
and compiles/vets affected Go packages without running test bodies, package-wide
Go linters, or full workflow recompilation. Run <code>make agent-report-progress</code>
once before the final push to retain the build, lint, impacted-test, and workflow
drift checks. Cold Go compilation and final validation can take longer than the
warm inner loop.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🏗️ Project Structure</h2><a id="user-content-️-project-structure" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🏗️ Project Structure" href="#️-project-structure"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📋 Dependency License Policy</h2><a id="user-content--dependency-license-policy" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📋 Dependency License Policy" href="#-dependency-license-policy"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This project uses an MIT license and only accepts dependencies with compatible licenses.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Allowed Licenses</h3><a id="user-content-allowed-licenses" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Allowed Licenses" href="#allowed-licenses"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The following open-source licenses are compatible with our MIT license:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>MIT</strong> - Most permissive, allows reuse with minimal restrictions</li>
<li><strong>Apache-2.0</strong> - Permissive license with patent grant</li>
<li><strong>BSD-2-Clause, BSD-3-Clause</strong> - Simple permissive licenses</li>
<li><strong>ISC</strong> - Simplified permissive license similar to MIT</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Disallowed Licenses</h3><a id="user-content-disallowed-licenses" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Disallowed Licenses" href="#disallowed-licenses"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The following licenses are <strong>not allowed</strong> as they conflict with our MIT license or impose unacceptable restrictions:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>GPL, LGPL, AGPL</strong> - Copyleft licenses that would force us to release under GPL</li>
<li><strong>SSPL</strong> - Server Side Public License with restrictive requirements</li>
<li><strong>Proprietary/Commercial</strong> - Closed-source licenses requiring payment or special terms</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Container Base OS Packages</h3><a id="user-content-container-base-os-packages" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Container Base OS Packages" href="#container-base-os-packages"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The license policy in <code>.grant.yaml</code> is also applied to the container images referenced by compiled workflows (<code>gh aw compile --grant</code>). Packages that ship with the upstream base images are listed under <code>ignore-packages</code> as a documented exception: the Alpine base OS packages (<code>busybox</code>, <code>apk-tools</code>, <code>alpine-baselayout</code>, <code>musl-utils</code>, <code>git</code>, <code>libgcc</code>, <code>libstdc++</code>, and their variants), the Debian base OS packages of the <code>ghcr.io/github/github-mcp-server</code> image (<code>base-files</code>, <code>libc6</code>, <code>libssl3</code>, <code>media-types</code>, <code>netbase</code>, <code>tzdata</code>), and the Node.js/npm runtime with npm's bundled dependencies (<code>node</code>, <code>npm</code>, <code>tar</code>, <code>glob</code>, <code>minipass</code>, and friends). They are executed as part of a third-party image, never linked into or redistributed with gh-aw, and cannot be changed without replacing the upstream image. Every other package in those images is still evaluated against the allowlist above.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>.grant.yaml</code> also supports an <code>ignore-images</code> list of glob patterns matched against the image reference. It excludes an entire image from license scanning and is reserved for third-party images whose bundled toolchains produce license findings that cannot be acted on, such as <code>ghcr.io/oraios/serena</code> (a Debian development image whose cargo registry ships hundreds of crates without SPDX license metadata). This key is specific to gh-aw; grant ignores it. Vulnerability scanning still covers every image, including the ignored ones.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>libatomic</code> is an explicitly approved runtime-package exception for the vendored <code>ghcr.io/github/gh-aw-node</code> image. The verified Linux/amd64 image inherited Alpine 3.24's <code>libatomic@15.2.0-r5</code> unchanged from its Node base. Its <a href="https://pkgs.alpinelinux.org/package/v3.24/main/x86_64/libatomic" rel="nofollow">Alpine package metadata</a> declares <code>GPL-2.0-or-later AND LGPL-2.1-or-later</code>, inherited from the GCC packaging recipe; <a href="https://github.com/gcc-mirror/gcc/blob/releases/gcc-15.2.0/libatomic/gload.c#L1-L24">GCC 15.2 libatomic source notices</a> specify <code>GPL-3.0-or-later WITH GCC-exception-3.1</code>. This is a policy acceptance, not a finding that publishing the runtime has no license obligations: the <a href="https://github.com/gcc-mirror/gcc/blob/releases/gcc-15.2.0/COPYING.RUNTIME">GCC Runtime Library Exception</a> does not waive applicable notice or source-distribution requirements for the library itself. Grant's <code>ignore-packages</code> mechanism applies by package name across scanned images and versions. The general license allowlist, vulnerability scanning, and vendored-image gates remain unchanged.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Container Vulnerability Exceptions</h3><a id="user-content-container-vulnerability-exceptions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Container Vulnerability Exceptions" href="#container-vulnerability-exceptions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>gh aw compile --grype</code> reads the optional <code>.grype.yaml</code> file at the repository root and applies its <code>ignore</code> rules. The file is reserved for documented risk acceptances: findings that have no fixed version available upstream, where there is nothing to upgrade to. Each rule is scoped to a specific vulnerability ID, package, and affected version so newly disclosed vulnerabilities and rebuilt packages are still reported, and each carries a <code>reason</code> explaining the acceptance. Remove a rule as soon as the upstream image ships a fix — the daily container scan runs <code>gh aw compile --force-refresh-container-pins</code> and picks up rebuilt base images automatically. When <code>.grype.yaml</code> is absent, grype runs with its defaults.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Before Adding a Dependency</h3><a id="user-content-before-adding-a-dependency" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Before Adding a Dependency" href="#before-adding-a-dependency"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">GitHub Copilot Agent automatically checks licenses when adding dependencies. However, if you're evaluating a dependency:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Check its license</strong>: Run <code>make license-check</code> after adding the dependency</li>
<li><strong>Review the report</strong>: Run <code>make license-report</code> to generate a CSV of all licenses</li>
<li><strong>If unsure</strong>: Ask in your PR - maintainers will help evaluate edge cases</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">License Checking</h3><a id="user-content-license-checking" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: License Checking" href="#license-checking"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The project includes automated license compliance checking:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>CI Workflow</strong>: <code>.github/workflows/license-check.yml</code> runs on every PR that changes <code>go.mod</code></li>
<li><strong>Local Check</strong>: Run <code>make license-check</code> to verify all dependencies (installs <code>go-licenses</code> on-demand)</li>
<li><strong>License Report</strong>: Run <code>make license-report</code> to see detailed license information</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">All dependencies are automatically scanned using Google's <code>go-licenses</code> tool in CI, which classifies licenses by type and identifies potential compliance issues. Note that <code>go-licenses</code> is not actively maintained, so we install it on-demand rather than as a regular build dependency.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🤖 Automated Dependency Updates (Dependabot)</h2><a id="user-content--automated-dependency-updates-dependabot" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🤖 Automated Dependency Updates (Dependabot)" href="#-automated-dependency-updates-dependabot"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This project uses GitHub Dependabot to automatically keep dependencies up-to-date with weekly security patches and version updates.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What Dependabot Monitors</h3><a id="user-content-what-dependabot-monitors" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What Dependabot Monitors" href="#what-dependabot-monitors"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Dependabot is configured in <code>.github/dependabot.yml</code> to monitor:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Go modules</strong> (<code>/go.mod</code>) - Weekly updates for Go dependencies</li>
<li><strong>npm packages</strong> - Weekly updates for:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Documentation site (<code>/docs/package.json</code>)</li>
<li>GitHub Actions setup scripts (<code>/actions/setup/js/package.json</code>)</li>
<li>Workflow dependencies (<code>/.github/workflows/package.json</code>)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Python packages</strong> (<code>/.github/workflows/requirements.txt</code>) - Weekly updates for workflow scripts</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Expected Behavior</h3><a id="user-content-expected-behavior" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Expected Behavior" href="#expected-behavior"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Schedule</strong>: Dependabot checks for updates <strong>every Monday</strong> (weekly interval)</li>
<li><strong>Pull Requests</strong>: Creates automated PRs from <code>dependabot[bot]</code> for:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Security vulnerabilities (immediate)</li>
<li>Version updates (weekly batch)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Limit</strong>: Maximum of 10 open PRs per ecosystem to prevent overwhelming maintainers</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What to Expect from Dependabot PRs</h3><a id="user-content-what-to-expect-from-dependabot-prs" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What to Expect from Dependabot PRs" href="#what-to-expect-from-dependabot-prs"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Dependabot PRs will:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Have clear titles like "Bump lodash from 4.17.20 to 4.17.21 in /docs"</li>
<li>Include changelog links and release notes</li>
<li>Show compatibility score based on semantic versioning</li>
<li>Automatically rebase when the base branch changes</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Troubleshooting Dependabot</h3><a id="user-content-troubleshooting-dependabot" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Troubleshooting Dependabot" href="#troubleshooting-dependabot"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If Dependabot stops creating PRs:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Check repository settings</strong>: Go to Settings → Security → Dependabot</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Ensure "Dependabot alerts" is enabled</li>
<li>Ensure "Dependabot security updates" is enabled</li>
<li>Ensure "Dependabot version updates" is enabled</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Verify configuration</strong>: Check <code>.github/dependabot.yml</code> syntax</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Directory paths must match locations of dependency files</li>
<li>Ecosystem names must be exact: <code>gomod</code>, <code>npm</code>, <code>pip</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Check for rate limits</strong>: Dependabot may be rate-limited if there are too many updates</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Manual trigger</strong>: You can manually trigger Dependabot from repository Settings → Security → Dependabot</p>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Handling Dependabot PRs</h3><a id="user-content-handling-dependabot-prs" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Handling Dependabot PRs" href="#handling-dependabot-prs"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When reviewing Dependabot PRs:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Review the changes</strong>: Check the changelog and compatibility score</li>
<li><strong>Let CI run</strong>: Wait for all GitHub Actions checks to pass</li>
<li><strong>Test if needed</strong>: For major version updates, test locally or let the agent verify</li>
<li><strong>Merge quickly</strong>: Security updates should be merged as soon as CI passes</li>
<li><strong>Batch updates</strong>: For minor version updates, you can merge multiple PRs at once</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security Patches</h3><a id="user-content-security-patches" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security Patches" href="#security-patches"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Dependabot prioritizes security patches:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Security vulnerabilities are updated <strong>immediately</strong> (not weekly)</li>
<li>PRs are tagged with severity level (critical, high, medium, low)</li>
<li>Security PRs should be reviewed and merged within 24-48 hours</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🧪 Testing</h2><a id="user-content--testing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🧪 Testing" href="#-testing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For comprehensive testing guidelines including assert vs require usage, table-driven test patterns, and best practices, see <strong><a href="/PawarisMopha/gh-aw/blob/main/scratchpad/testing.md">scratchpad/testing.md</a></strong>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Quick reference:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>make test-unit</code> - Impacted Go tests (30-second selected-runtime budget; compilation is additional)</li>
<li><code>make test-unit-all</code> - Full Go unit test suite</li>
<li><code>make agent-report-progress</code> - Final branch-scoped validation before pushing</li>
<li><code>make agent-finish</code> - Explicit repository-wide maintenance sweep; not an inner-loop command</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Compiling against a different actions repository</h3><a id="user-content-compiling-against-a-different-actions-repository" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Compiling against a different actions repository" href="#compiling-against-a-different-actions-repository"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When working on changes that span both <code>github/gh-aw</code> and <code>github/gh-aw-actions</code>, you can compile workflows against a fork or branch of the actions repository:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Compile against a fork with a specific branch
./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \
  --actions-repo myorg/my-aw-actions \
  --action-tag my-feature-branch \
  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md

# Compile with a specific tag or SHA
./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \
  --action-tag abc123def456 \
  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Compile against a fork with a specific branch</span>
./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \
  --actions-repo myorg/my-aw-actions \
  --action-tag my-feature-branch \
  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Compile with a specific tag or SHA</span>
./gh-aw compile --action-mode action \
  --action-tag abc123def456 \
  .github/workflows/my-workflow.md</pre></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>--action-mode action</code> — Required with <code>--actions-repo</code>. References actions as GitHub Actions from an external repository instead of inlining scripts locally.</li>
<li><code>--actions-repo &lt;owner/repo&gt;</code> — Override the default <code>github/gh-aw-actions</code> repository.</li>
<li><code>--action-tag &lt;tag-or-sha&gt;</code> — Pin action references to a specific tag, branch, or commit SHA.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Use these flags when testing workflow behavior against a branch in <code>github/gh-aw-actions</code> before a release is cut.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚫 Spam Prevention</h2><a id="user-content--spam-prevention" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚫 Spam Prevention" href="#-spam-prevention"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Be nice, don't spam.</strong> The project maintainers reserve the right to clean up spam, unsolicited promotions, or off-topic content as needed to keep discussions focused and valuable for all contributors.</p>
<p dir="auto">This includes but is not limited to:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Repeated identical or similar comments across multiple issues or pull requests</li>
<li>Unsolicited promotional content or advertisements</li>
<li>Off-topic comments that don't contribute to the discussion</li>
<li>Automated bot comments without prior approval</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🤝 Community</h2><a id="user-content--community" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🤝 Community" href="#-community"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
